1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the elements structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 723. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 227 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the elements space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 723 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 227. QED.
